{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"reasons","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The reason this tree fell is that it had rotted.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A cause:","That which causes something: an efficient cause, a proximate cause."],"synonyms":[{"word":"occasion"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He made reason with his boss about taking a day off.","type":"example"},{"text":"The reason I robbed the bank was that I needed the money.","type":"example"},{"text":"If you don't give me a reason to go with you, I won't.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A cause:","A motive for an action or a determination."],"synonyms":[{"word":"occasion"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A cause:","An excuse: a thought or a consideration offered in support of a determination or an opinion; the cognitive faculties, collectively, of conception, judgment, deduction and intuition."],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Something reasonable, in accordance with thought; justice."],"tags":["countable","obsolete","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Ratio; {"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"reasons","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"reasoning","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"reasoned","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"reasoned","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To deduce or come to a conclusion by being rational."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To perform a process of deduction or of induction, in order to convince or to confute; to argue."],"synonyms":[{"word":"ratiocinate"}],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To converse; to compare opinions."],"tags":["intransitive","obsolete"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I reasoned the matter with my friend.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To arrange and present the reasons for or against; to examine or discuss by arguments; to debate or discuss."],"tags":["ambitransitive"]},{"glosses":["To support with reasons, as a request."],"tags":["rare","transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to reason one into a belief; to reason one out of his plan","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To persuade by reasoning or argument."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to reason down a passion","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To overcome or conquer by adducing reasons."],"tags":["transitive","with-down"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to reason out the causes of the librations of the moon","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To find by logical process; to explain or justify by reason or argument."],"raw_tags":["with out"],"tags":["transitive","usually"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈɹiːzən/"},{"ipa":"[ˈɹʷɪi̯zən] ~ [ˈɹʷɪi̯zn̩]"}],"upstream_line":47062,"upstream_offset":314125671,"upstream_record_sha256":"5c86941d67e5938742a77100fc80078e4ce2cbb282bba95178911192e359f0a1","word":"reason"}
